PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan the logistics of transporting crates between a number of depots and distributors via trucks that are loaded by hoists. Depots and distributors are directly connected by roads (trucks can drive between any two depots or distributors).

A depot is a type of place.
A distributor is a type of place.
A pallet is a type of surface.
A crate is a type of surface.

Here are the actions that can be performed:

Drive a truck from one place to another place.
Use a hoist to lift a crate from a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to drop a crate to a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to load a crate into a truck at a place.
Use a hoist to unload a crate from a truck at a place.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A truck can be driven from one place to another place only if the truck is at the origin place.
Once a truck has been driven from one place to another, it is not at the origin place and is at the destination place.
A crate can be lifted by a hoist only if the hoist is at the same place as the crate, the hoist is available, and the crate is clear.
Once a crate has been lifted by a hoist from a surface at a place, the crate is not at the place, the hoist is lifting the crate, the hoist is not available, the surface is clear, and the crate is not on the surface.
A crate can be dropped by a hoist to a surface only if the hoist and surface are both at the place, the surface is clear, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been dropped by a hoist to a surface at a place, the hoist is available, the hoist is not lifting the crate, the crate is at the place, the surface is not clear, the crate is clear, and the crate is on the surface.
A crate can be loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place, the truck is at the same place, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place, 
A crate can be unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place as the truck, the hoist is available, and the crate is in the truck.
Once a crate has been unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place, the crate is not in the truck, the hoist is not available, and the hoist is lifting the crate.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, crate0 is at depot1, crate1 is at depot1, crate2 is at depot0, hoist0 is at depot0, hoist1 is at depot1, hoist2 is at depot2, hoist3 is at distributor0, pallet0 is at depot0, pallet1 is at depot1, pallet2 is at depot2, pallet3 is at distributor0, truck0 is at depot1, truck1 is at depot1, truck2 is at distributor0, hoist0 is available, hoist1 is available, hoist2 is available, hoist3 is available, crate1 is clear, crate2 is clear, pallet2 is clear, pallet3 is clear, crate0 is on pallet1, crate1 is on crate0 and crate2 is on pallet0.
My goal is to have that crate1 is on pallet1 and crate2 is on pallet3.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
I have to plan the logistics of transporting crates between a number of depots and distributors via trucks that are loaded by hoists. Depots and distributors are directly connected by roads (trucks can drive between any two depots or distributors).

A depot is a type of place.
A distributor is a type of place.
A pallet is a type of surface.
A crate is a type of surface.

Here are the actions that can be performed:

Drive a truck from one place to another place.
Use a hoist to lift a crate from a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to drop a crate to a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to load a crate into a truck at a place.
Use a hoist to unload a crate from a truck at a place.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A truck can be driven from one place to another place only if the truck is at the origin place.
Once a truck has been driven from one place to another, it is not at the origin place and is at the destination place.
A crate can be lifted by a hoist only if the hoist is at the same place as the crate, the hoist is available, and the crate is clear.
Once a crate has been lifted by a hoist from a surface at a place, the crate is not at the place, the hoist is lifting the crate, the hoist is not available, the surface is clear, and the crate is not on the surface.
A crate can be dropped by a hoist to a surface only if the hoist and surface are both at the place, the surface is clear, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been dropped by a hoist to a surface at a place, the hoist is available, the hoist is not lifting the crate, the crate is at the place, the surface is not clear, the crate is clear, and the crate is on the surface.
A crate can be loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place, the truck is at the same place, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place, 
A crate can be unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place as the truck, the hoist is available, and the crate is in the truck.
Once a crate has been unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place, the crate is not in the truck, the hoist is not available, and the hoist is lifting the crate.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, crate0 is at depot1, crate1 is at depot1, crate2 is at depot0, hoist0 is at depot0, hoist1 is at depot1, hoist2 is at depot2, hoist3 is at distributor0, pallet0 is at depot0, pallet1 is at depot1, pallet2 is at depot2, pallet3 is at distributor0, truck0 is at depot1, truck1 is at depot1, truck2 is at distributor0, hoist0 is available, hoist1 is available, hoist2 is available, hoist3 is available, crate1 is clear, crate2 is clear, pallet2 is clear, pallet3 is clear, crate0 is on pallet1, crate1 is on crate0 and crate2 is on pallet0.
My goal is to have that crate1 is on pallet1 and crate2 is on pallet3.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]
================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
<domain>
```pddl
(define (domain logistics-depot-combined)
    (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
    (:types
        location - object     ; General place, unifying 'place' and 'location'
        vehicle - object      ; General mobile transport unit
        truck - vehicle       ; Specific type of vehicle
        item - object         ; General object to be moved
        crate - item          ; Specific type of item
        hoist - object        ; Equipment for lifting/moving items within a location
    )

    (:predicates
        ;; Predicates for item and vehicle locations
        (at ?i - item ?l - location)          ; Item ?i is physically located at location ?l (on the ground)
        (located-at ?v - vehicle ?l - location); Vehicle ?v is positioned at location ?l (e.g., a loading bay, or depot)

        ;; Predicates for connections and space
        (connected ?from ?to - location)      ; Indicates a direct road connection exists between two locations.
        (location-clear ?l - location)        ; Location ?l's ground is clear and available to receive an item
        (vehicle-has-space ?v - vehicle)      ; Vehicle ?v has available capacity to load at least one more item

        ;; Predicates for hoist and item handling
        (hoist-free ?h - hoist)               ; Hoist ?h is not currently holding any item
        (holding ?h - hoist ?i - item)        ; Hoist ?h is currently holding item ?i
        (in-vehicle ?i - item ?v - vehicle)   ; Item ?i is currently loaded inside vehicle ?v
    )

    ;; Action: Drive (Modified from Logistics Planner)
    ;; This action models a truck driving from a starting location to a destination location.
    ;; It only moves the truck, explicit load/unload actions are required for items.
    (:action drive
        :parameters (?t - truck ?from ?to - location)
        :precondition (and
            (located-at ?t ?from)         ; The truck must be present at the starting location.
            (connected ?from ?to)         ; A valid road connection must exist.
        )
        :effect (and
            (not (located-at ?t ?from))   ; The truck is no longer at the starting location.
            (located-at ?t ?to)           ; The truck is now at the destination location.
        )
    )

    ;; Action: Lift (from Depot Manager)
    ;; Lifts an item from a ground location using a hoist.
    (:action lift
        :parameters (?h - hoist ?i - item ?l - location)
        :precondition (and
            (at ?i ?l)
            (hoist-free ?h)
        )
        :effect (and
            (not (at ?i ?l))
            (not (hoist-free ?h))
            (holding ?h ?i)
            (location-clear ?l)
        )
    )

    ;; Action: Drop (from Depot Manager)
    ;; Drops an item from a hoist to a ground location.
    (:action drop
        :parameters (?h - hoist ?i - item ?l - location)
        :precondition (and
            (holding ?h ?i)
            (location-clear ?l)
        )
        :effect (and
            (not (holding ?h ?i))
            (hoist-free ?h)
            (at ?i ?l)
            (not (location-clear ?l))
        )
    )

    ;; Action: Load (from Depot Manager)
    ;; Loads an item from a hoist into a vehicle.
    (:action load
        :parameters (?h - hoist ?i - item ?v - vehicle ?l - location) ; ?l is the location where the vehicle is
        :precondition (and
            (holding ?h ?i)
            (located-at ?v ?l) ; Vehicle must be at the loading bay
            (vehicle-has-space ?v)
        )
        :effect (and
            (not (holding ?h ?i))
            (hoist-free ?h)
            (in-vehicle ?i ?v)
            (not (vehicle-has-space ?v))
        )
    )

    ;; Action: Unload (from Depot Manager)
    ;; Unloads an item from a vehicle using a hoist.
    (:action unload
        :parameters (?h - hoist ?i - item ?v - vehicle ?l - location) ; ?l is the location where the vehicle is
        :precondition (and
            (hoist-free ?h)
            (in-vehicle ?i ?v)
            (located-at ?v ?l) ; Vehicle must be at the unloading bay
        )
        :effect (and
            (not (in-vehicle ?i ?v))
            (holding ?h ?i)
            (not (hoist-free ?h))
            (vehicle-has-space ?v)
        )
    )
)
```
</domain>

<problem>
```pddl
(define (problem unified-logistics-depot)
    (:domain logistics-depot-combined)
    (:objects
        depot0 distributor0 spot1 spot2 bay1 pallet1 pallet3 - location
        truck0 truck1 - truck
        crate1 crate2 - crate
        itemA - item
        hoist1 - hoist
    )
    (:init
        ;; Initial locations and states from Logistics Planner (adapted)
        (located-at truck0 depot0)      ; Truck0 starts at depot0.
        (at crate2 depot0)              ; Crate2 starts at depot0 (on the ground).
        (connected depot0 distributor0) ; Road connection.
        (connected distributor0 depot0) ; Bidirectional connection.

        ;; Added for crate1 and pallets, inferred connectivity
        (at crate1 spot2)               ; Crate1 starts at spot2 (on the ground).
        (connected distributor0 pallet1)
        (connected pallet1 distributor0)
        (connected distributor0 pallet3)
        (connected pallet3 distributor0)
        (connected spot2 distributor0)  ; Assuming spot2 is connected to the distributor for movement
        (connected distributor0 spot2)  ; Assuming bidirectional connection

        ;; Initial states from Depot Manager (adapted)
        (hoist-free hoist1)             ; Hoist1 is not holding any item.
        (at itemA spot1)                ; ItemA is initially at spot1 (on the ground).
        (located-at truck1 bay1)        ; Truck1 is positioned at bay1.
        (vehicle-has-space truck1)      ; Truck1 is empty and ready to receive an item.

        ;; Initial location clear/occupancy states (adapted and inferred)
        (not (location-clear depot0))   ; depot0 is not clear because crate2 is at it.
        (location-clear distributor0)   ; distributor0 is initially clear.
        (not (location-clear spot1))    ; spot1 is not clear because itemA is at it.
        (not (location-clear spot2))    ; spot2 is not clear because crate1 is at it.
        (location-clear bay1)           ; bay1's ground is clear (truck is located-at it, not at it as an item).
        (location-clear pallet1)        ; pallet1 is initially clear.
        (location-clear pallet3)        ; pallet3 is initially clear.

        ;; Inferred initial states for truck0 based on harmonized predicates
        (vehicle-has-space truck0)      ; Assuming truck0 is empty and has space.
    )
    (:goal (and
        (at crate1 pallet1)
        (at crate2 pallet3)
    ))
)
```
</problem>
================================================================================

```pddl
(define (domain logistics-depot-combined)
    (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
    (:types
        location - object     ; General place, unifying 'place' and 'location'
        vehicle - object      ; General mobile transport unit
        truck - vehicle       ; Specific type of vehicle
        item - object         ; General object to be moved
        crate - item          ; Specific type of item
        hoist - object        ; Equipment for lifting/moving items within a location
    )

    (:predicates
        ;; Predicates for item and vehicle locations
        (at ?i - item ?l - location)          ; Item ?i is physically located at location ?l (on the ground)
        (located-at ?v - vehicle ?l - location); Vehicle ?v is positioned at location ?l (e.g., a loading bay, or depot)

        ;; Predicates for connections and space
        (connected ?from ?to - location)      ; Indicates a direct road connection exists between two locations.
        (location-clear ?l - location)        ; Location ?l's ground is clear and available to receive an item
        (vehicle-has-space ?v - vehicle)      ; Vehicle ?v has available capacity to load at least one more item

        ;; Predicates for hoist and item handling
        (hoist-free ?h - hoist)               ; Hoist ?h is not currently holding any item
        (holding ?h - hoist ?i - item)        ; Hoist ?h is currently holding item ?i
        (in-vehicle ?i - item ?v - vehicle)   ; Item ?i is currently loaded inside vehicle ?v
    )

    ;; Action: Drive (Modified from Logistics Planner)
    ;; This action models a truck driving from a starting location to a destination location.
    ;; It only moves the truck, explicit load/unload actions are required for items.
    (:action drive
        :parameters (?t - truck ?from ?to - location)
        :precondition (and
            (located-at ?t ?from)         ; The truck must be present at the starting location.
            (connected ?from ?to)         ; A valid road connection must exist.
        )
        :effect (and
            (not (located-at ?t ?from))   ; The truck is no longer at the starting location.
            (located-at ?t ?to)           ; The truck is now at the destination location.
        )
    )

    ;; Action: Lift (from Depot Manager)
    ;; Lifts an item from a ground location using a hoist.
    (:action lift
        :parameters (?h - hoist ?i - item ?l - location)
        :precondition (and
            (at ?i ?l)
            (hoist-free ?h)
        )
        :effect (and
            (not (at ?i ?l))
            (not (hoist-free ?h))
            (holding ?h ?i)
            (location-clear ?l)
        )
    )

    ;; Action: Drop (from Depot Manager)
    ;; Drops an item from a hoist to a ground location.
    (:action drop
        :parameters (?h - hoist ?i - item ?l - location)
        :precondition (and
            (holding ?h ?i)
            (location-clear ?l)
        )
        :effect (and
            (not (holding ?h ?i))
            (hoist-free ?h)
            (at ?i ?l)
            (not (location-clear ?l))
        )
    )

    ;; Action: Load (from Depot Manager)
    ;; Loads an item from a hoist into a vehicle.
    (:action load
        :parameters (?h - hoist ?i - item ?v - vehicle ?l - location) ; ?l is the location where the vehicle is
        :precondition (and
            (holding ?h ?i)
            (located-at ?v ?l) ; Vehicle must be at the loading bay
            (vehicle-has-space ?v)
        )
        :effect (and
            (not (holding ?h ?i))
            (hoist-free ?h)
            (in-vehicle ?i ?v)
            (not (vehicle-has-space ?v))
        )
    )

    ;; Action: Unload (from Depot Manager)
    ;; Unloads an item from a vehicle using a hoist.
    (:action unload
        :parameters (?h - hoist ?i - item ?v - vehicle ?l - location) ; ?l is the location where the vehicle is
        :precondition (and
            (hoist-free ?h)
            (in-vehicle ?i ?v)
            (located-at ?v ?l) ; Vehicle must be at the unloading bay
        )
        :effect (and
            (not (in-vehicle ?i ?v))
            (holding ?h ?i)
            (not (hoist-free ?h))
            (vehicle-has-space ?v)
        )
    )
)

General mobile transport unit\n        truck - vehicle       ; Specific type of vehicle\n        item - object         ; General object to be moved\n        crate - item          ; Specific type of item\n        hoist - object        ; Equipment for lifting/moving items within a location\n    )\n\n    (:predicates\n        ;; Predicates for item and vehicle locations\n        (at ?i - item ?l - location)          ; Item ?i is physically located at location ?l (on the ground)\n        (located-at ?v - vehicle ?l - location); Vehicle ?v is positioned at location ?l (e.g., a loading bay, or depot)\n\n        ;; Predicates for connections and space\n        (connected ?from ?to - location)      ; Indicates a direct road connection exists between two locations.\n        (location-clear ?l - location)        ; Location ?l's ground is clear and available to receive an item\n        (vehicle-has-space ?v - vehicle)      ; Vehicle ?v has available capacity to load at least one more item\n\n        ;; Predicates for hoist and item handling\n        (hoist-free ?h - hoist)               ; Hoist ?h is not currently holding any item\n        (holding ?h - hoist ?i - item)        ; Hoist ?h is currently holding item ?i\n        (in-vehicle ?i - item ?v - vehicle)   ; Item ?i is currently loaded inside vehicle ?v\n    )\n\n    ;; The truck must be present at the starting location.\n            (connected ?from ?to)         ; A valid road connection must exist.\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (located-at ?t ?from))   ;     "pddl_domain": "(define (domain logistics-depot-combined)\n    (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)\n    (:types\n        place\n        depot - place\n        distributor - place\n        surface\n        pallet - surface\n        crate - surface ; Stacking relationship: ?c is on top of ?s\n        (on ?c - crate ?s - surface)\n        \n        ;; Clearness: ?s has nothing on top of it\n        (clear ?s - surface)\n        \n        ;; Crate inside a truck\n        (in_truck ?c - crate ?t - truck)\n        \n        ;; Hoist availability\n        (hoist_available ?h - hoist)\n        \n        ;; Hoist lifting a specific crate\n        (hoist_lifting ?h - hoist ?c - crate)\n        \n        ;; Road connectivity between places\n        (connected ?from ?to - place)\n    )\n\n    ;; Action: Drive a truck from one place to another place.\n    (:action drive\n        :parameters (?t - truck ?from ?to - place)\n        :precondition (and\n            (at ?t ?from)\n            (connected ?from ?to)\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (at ?t ?from))\n            (at ?t ?to)\n        )\n    )\n\n    ;; Action: Use a hoist to lift a crate from a surface at a place.\n    (:action lift\n        :parameters (?h - hoist ?c - crate ?s - surface ?p - place)\n        :precondition (and\n            (at ?h ?p)           ; Hoist must be at the place\n            (at ?c ?p)           ; Crate must be at the place\n            (on ?c ?s)           ; Crate must be on the specified surface\n            (hoist_available ?h) ; Hoist must be free\n            (clear ?c)           ; Crate itself must be clear to be lifted\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (hoist_available ?h))\n            (hoist_lifting ?h ?c)\n            (not (at ?c ?p))     ; Crate is no longer at the place (it's lifted)\n            (not (on ?c ?s))     ; Crate is no longer on the surface\n            (clear ?s)           ; The surface it was on is now clear\n        )\n    )\n\n    ;; Action: Use a hoist to drop a crate to a surface at a place.\n    (:action drop\n        :parameters (?h - hoist ?c - crate ?s - surface ?p - place)\n        :precondition (and\n            (at ?h ?p)           ; Hoist must be at the place\n            (at ?s ?p)           ; Target surface must be at the place\n            (hoist_lifting ?h ?c) ; Hoist must be lifting the crate\n            (clear ?s)           ; Target surface must be clear\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (hoist_available ?h)\n            (not (hoist_lifting ?h ?c))\n            (at ?c ?p)           ; Crate is now at the place on the ground/surface\n            (on ?c ?s)           ; Crate is now on the surface\n            (not (clear ?s))     ; Target surface is no longer clear\n            (clear ?c)           ; Crate itself is clear after being dropped (nothing on top of it)\n        )\n    )\n\n    ;; This is implied by (hoist_lifting ?h ?c) if a crate can only be in one state.\n                                  ; Adding it doesn't hurt, but the current problem doesn't need it.\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (hoist_lifting ?h ?c))\n            (hoist_available ?h)\n            (in_truck ?c ?t)\n        )\n    )\n\n    ;; Action: Use a hoist to unload a crate from a truck at a place.\n    (:action unload\n        :parameters (?h - hoist ?c - crate ?t - truck ?p - place)\n        :precondition (and\n            (at ?h ?p)           ; Crate must be in the truck\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (in_truck ?c ?t))\n            (not (hoist_available ?h))\n            (hoist_lifting ?h ?c) ; Hoist is now lifting the crate\n        )\n    )\n)",

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan the logistics of transporting crates between a number of depots and distributors via trucks that are loaded by hoists. Depots and distributors are directly connected by roads (trucks can drive between any two depots or distributors).

A depot is a type of place.
A distributor is a type of place.
A pallet is a type of surface.
A crate is a type of surface.

Here are the actions that can be performed:

Drive a truck from one place to another place.
Use a hoist to lift a crate from a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to drop a crate to a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to load a crate into a truck at a place.
Use a hoist to unload a crate from a truck at a place.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A truck can be driven from one place to another place only if the truck is at the origin place.
Once a truck has been driven from one place to another, it is not at the origin place and is at the destination place.
A crate can be lifted by a hoist only if the hoist is at the same place as the crate, the hoist is available, and the crate is clear.
Once a crate has been lifted by a hoist from a surface at a place, the crate is not at the place, the hoist is lifting the crate, the hoist is not available, the surface is clear, and the crate is not on the surface.
A crate can be dropped by a hoist to a surface only if the hoist and surface are both at the place, the surface is clear, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been dropped by a hoist to a surface at a place, the hoist is available, the hoist is not lifting the crate, the crate is at the place, the surface is not clear, the crate is clear, and the crate is on the surface.
A crate can be loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place, the truck is at the same place, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place, 
A crate can be unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place as the truck, the hoist is available, and the crate is in the truck.
Once a crate has been unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place, the crate is not in the truck, the hoist is not available, and the hoist is lifting the crate.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, crate0 is at depot0, crate1 is at depot0, crate2 is at depot2, hoist0 is at depot0, hoist1 is at depot1, hoist2 is at depot2, hoist3 is at distributor0, pallet0 is at depot0, pallet1 is at depot1, pallet2 is at depot2, pallet3 is at distributor0, truck0 is at depot0, truck1 is at depot1, truck2 is at depot0, hoist0 is available, hoist1 is available, hoist2 is available, hoist3 is available, crate1 is clear, crate2 is clear, pallet1 is clear, pallet3 is clear, crate0 is on pallet0, crate1 is on crate0 and crate2 is on pallet2.
My goal is to have that crate0 is on pallet3, crate1 is on pallet0 and crate2 is on crate0.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]

    "pddl_domain": "(define (domain depots1)\n    (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)\n    (:types\n        place\n        locatable_item ; Supertype for anything that can be located at a 'place'\n        surface - locatable_item ; Surfaces (pallets, crates) are also locatable items\n\n        depot distributor - place\n        pallet crate - surface\n        truck hoist - locatable_item\n    )\n    (:predicates\n        (at ?obj - locatable_item ?p - place)\n        (hoist_available ?h - hoist)\n        (hoist_lifting ?h - hoist ?c - crate)\n        (clear ?s - surface) ; True if nothing is stacked on ?s\n        (on ?c - crate ?s - surface) ; ?c is directly on top of ?s\n        (in_truck ?c - crate ?t - truck) ; ?c is inside ?t\n    )\n\n    (:action drive_truck\n        :parameters (?t - truck ?p_from - place ?p_to - place)\n        :precondition (and (at ?t ?p_from)\n                           (not (= ?p_from ?p_to)))\n        :effect (and (not (at ?t ?p_from))\n                     (at ?t ?p_to))\n    )\n\n    (:action lift_crate_from_surface\n        :parameters (?h - hoist ?c - crate ?s - surface ?p - place)\n        :precondition (and (at ?h ?p)\n                           (at ?c ?p)\n                           (hoist_available ?h)\n                           (clear ?c) ; Crate must have nothing on it to be lifted\n                           (on ?c ?s) ; Crate must be on a surface\n                           (at ?s ?p) ; Surface must be at the same place\n                      )\n        :effect (and (not (at ?c ?p)) ; Crate is no longer at the place in a fixed position\n                     (hoist_lifting ?h ?c)\n                     (not (hoist_available ?h))\n                     (clear ?s) ; The surface ?s now has nothing on it\n                     (not (on ?c ?s)) ; Crate is no longer on the surface\n                )\n    )\n\n    (:action drop_crate_to_surface\n        :parameters (?h - hoist ?c - crate ?s - surface ?p - place)\n        :precondition (and (at ?h ?p)\n                           (at ?s ?p)\n                           (clear ?s) ; The target surface must be clear to receive a crate\n                           (hoist_lifting ?h ?c)\n                      )\n        :effect (and (hoist_available ?h)\n                     (not (hoist_lifting ?h ?c))\n                     (at ?c ?p) ; Crate is now at the place, fixed on surface\n                     (not (clear ?s)) ; The target surface is no longer clear\n                     (clear ?c) ; Crate itself is clear (nothing on it)\n                     (on ?c ?s)\n                )\n    )\n\n    (:action load_crate_into_truck\n        :parameters (?h - hoist ?c - crate ?t - truck ?p - place)\n        :precondition (and (at ?h ?p)\n                           (at ?t ?p)\n                           (hoist_lifting ?h ?c)\n                      )\n        :effect (and (in_truck ?c ?t)\n                     (hoist_available ?h)\n                     (not (hoist_lifting ?h ?c))\n                )\n    )\n\n    (:action unload_crate_from_truck\n        :parameters (?h - hoist ?c - crate ?t - truck ?p - place)\n        :precondition (and (at ?h ?p)\n                           (at ?t ?p)\n                           (hoist_available ?h) ; Hoist must be available to pick up from truck\n                           (in_truck ?c ?t)\n                      )\n        :effect (and (not (in_truck ?c ?t))\n                     (not (hoist_available ?h))\n                     (hoist_lifting ?h ?c)\n                )\n    )\n)",
    "pddl_problem": "(define (problem depots1-instance)\n    (:domain depots1)\n    (:objects\n        depot0 depot1 depot2 distributor0 - place\n        pallet0 pallet1 pallet2 pallet3 - pallet\n        crate0 crate1 crate2 - crate\n        truck0 truck1 truck2 - truck\n        hoist0 hoist1 hoist2 hoist3 - hoist\n    )\n    (:init\n        ; Locations of objects\n        (at crate0 depot0)\n        (at crate1 depot0)\n        (at crate2 depot2)\n\n        (at hoist0 depot0)\n        (at hoist1 depot1)\n        (at hoist2 depot2)\n        (at hoist3 distributor0)\n\n        (at pallet0 depot0)\n        (at pallet1 depot1)\n        (at pallet2 depot2)\n        (at pallet3 distributor0)\n\n        (at truck0 depot0)\n        (at truck1 depot1)\n        (at truck2 depot0)\n\n        ; Hoist availability\n        (hoist_available hoist0)\n        (hoist_available hoist1)\n        (hoist_available hoist2)\n        (hoist_available hoist3)\n\n        ; Stacking relationships and derived clear states\n        (on crate0 pallet0) ; crate0 on pallet0 -> pallet0 not clear\n        (on crate1 crate0) ; crate1 on crate0 -> crate0 not clear\n        (on crate2 pallet2) ; crate2 on pallet2 -> pallet2 not clear\n\n        (clear crate1) ; as stated (nothing on crate1)\n        (clear crate2) ; as stated (nothing on crate2)\n        (clear pallet1) ; as stated (nothing on pallet1)\n        (clear pallet3) ; as stated (nothing on pallet3)\n\n        (not (clear pallet0)) ; Inferred from (on crate0 pallet0)\n        (not (clear crate0)) ; Inferred from (on crate1 crate0)\n        (not (clear pallet2)) ; Inferred from (on crate2 pallet2)\n    )\n    (:goal (and\n        (on crate0 pallet3)\n        (on crate1 pallet0)\n        (on crate2 crate0)\n    ))",

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan the logistics of transporting crates between a number of depots and distributors via trucks that are loaded by hoists. Depots and distributors are directly connected by roads (trucks can drive between any two depots or distributors).

A depot is a type of place.
A distributor is a type of place.
A pallet is a type of surface.
A crate is a type of surface.

Here are the actions that can be performed:

Drive a truck from one place to another place.
Use a hoist to lift a crate from a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to drop a crate to a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to load a crate into a truck at a place.
Use a hoist to unload a crate from a truck at a place.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A truck can be driven from one place to another place only if the truck is at the origin place.
Once a truck has been driven from one place to another, it is not at the origin place and is at the destination place.
A crate can be lifted by a hoist only if the hoist is at the same place as the crate, the hoist is available, and the crate is clear.
Once a crate has been lifted by a hoist from a surface at a place, the crate is not at the place, the hoist is lifting the crate, the hoist is not available, the surface is clear, and the crate is not on the surface.
A crate can be dropped by a hoist to a surface only if the hoist and surface are both at the place, the surface is clear, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been dropped by a hoist to a surface at a place, the hoist is available, the hoist is not lifting the crate, the crate is at the place, the surface is not clear, the crate is clear, and the crate is on the surface.
A crate can be loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place, the truck is at the same place, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place, 
A crate can be unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place as the truck, the hoist is available, and the crate is in the truck.
Once a crate has been unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place, the crate is not in the truck, the hoist is not available, and the hoist is lifting the crate.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, crate0 is at depot2, crate1 is at depot0, crate2 is at depot2, hoist0 is at depot0, hoist1 is at depot1, hoist2 is at depot2, hoist3 is at distributor0, pallet0 is at depot0, pallet1 is at depot1, pallet2 is at depot2, pallet3 is at distributor0, truck0 is at depot2, truck1 is at depot0, truck2 is at depot2, hoist0 is available, hoist1 is available, hoist2 is available, hoist3 is available, crate1 is clear, crate2 is clear, pallet1 is clear, pallet3 is clear, crate0 is on pallet2, crate1 is on pallet0 and crate2 is on crate0.
My goal is to have that crate1 is on pallet0 and crate2 is on pallet3.

My plan is as follows:

    "pddl_domain": "(define (domain depots2)\n  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions :action-costs)\n  (:types\n    place truck hoist crate pallet - object\n  )\n  (:predicates\n    (at-truck ?t - truck ?p - place)\n    (at-hoist ?h - hoist ?p - place)\n    (at-pallet ?pa - pallet ?p - place)\n    (at-crate ?c - crate ?p - place)\n    (on ?c - crate ?s - (either crate pallet))\n    (clear ?s - (either crate pallet))\n    (holding ?h - hoist ?c - crate)\n    (in-truck ?c - crate ?t - truck)\n    (available ?h - hoist)\n  )\n  (:functions (total-cost))\n\n  ;; ACTIONS\n  (action drive\n    :parameters (?t - truck ?from - place ?to - place)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at-truck ?t ?from)\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at-truck ?t ?from))\n      (at-truck ?t ?to)\n      (increase (total-cost) 1)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (action lift-from-pallet\n    :parameters (?h - hoist ?c - crate ?pa - pallet ?p - place)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at-hoist ?h ?p)\n      (at-pallet ?pa ?p)\n      (at-crate ?c ?p)\n      (on ?c ?pa)\n      (clear ?c)\n      (available ?h)\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (holding ?h ?c)\n      (not (on ?c ?pa))\n      (not (at-crate ?c ?p))\n      (clear ?pa)\n      (not (available ?h))\n      (increase (total-cost) 1)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (action lift-from-crate\n    :parameters (?h - hoist ?c - crate ?s - crate ?p - place)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at-hoist ?h ?p)\n      (at-crate ?c ?p)\n      (at-crate ?s ?p)\n      (on ?c ?s)\n      (clear ?c)\n      (available ?h)\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (holding ?h ?c)\n      (not (on ?c ?s))\n      (not (at-crate ?c ?p))\n      (clear ?s)\n      (not (available ?h))\n      (increase (total-cost) 1)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (action drop-on-pallet\n    :parameters (?h - hoist ?c - crate ?pa - pallet ?p - place)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at-hoist ?h ?p)\n      (at-pallet ?pa ?p)\n      (holding ?h ?c)\n      (clear ?pa)\n      (not (available ?h)) ; Hoist is busy holding the crate\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (on ?c ?pa)\n      (at-crate ?c ?p)\n      (not (holding ?h ?c))\n      (not (clear ?pa))\n      (clear ?c)\n      (available ?h)\n      (increase (total-cost) 1)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (action drop-on-crate\n    :parameters (?h - hoist ?c - crate ?s - crate ?p - place)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at-hoist ?h ?p)\n      (at-crate ?s ?p)\n      (holding ?h ?c)\n      (clear ?s)\n      (not (available ?h)) ; Hoist is busy holding the crate\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (on ?c ?s)\n      (at-crate ?c ?p)\n      (not (holding ?h ?c))\n      (not (clear ?s))\n      (clear ?c)\n      (available ?h)\n      (increase (total-cost) 1)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (action load\n    :parameters (?h - hoist ?c - crate ?t - truck ?p - place)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at-hoist ?h ?p)\n      (at-truck ?t ?p)\n      (holding ?h ?c)\n      ; Hoist is busy holding the crate, so not available for another task.\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (in-truck ?c ?t)\n      (not (holding ?h ?c))\n      (available ?h)\n      (increase (total-cost) 1)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (action unload\n    :parameters (?h - hoist ?c - crate ?t - truck ?p - place)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at-hoist ?h ?p)\n      (at-truck ?t ?p)\n      (in-truck ?c ?t)\n      (available ?h) ; NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_4
------------------------------
Here is a sequence of human-readable actions, derived from the PDDL plan, to achieve the goal of having crate1 on pallet0 and crate2 on pallet3. Note that the plan does not contain specific time durations or timestamps.

**Overall Goal:** Coordinate transportation and loading activities to achieve the state where crate1 is on pallet0 AND crate2 is on pallet3.

**Action Plan:**

1.  **LoadingSpecialist Action:** At **depot2**, the LoadingSpecialist uses **hoist2** to lift **crate2** from **crate0**.
    *   *Initial State:* Crate2 is on crate0 at depot2. Hoist2 is available at depot2.
    *   *Result:* Hoist2 is now holding crate2. Crate0 is clear.

2.  **LoadingSpecialist Action:** At **depot2**, the LoadingSpecialist uses **hoist2** to load **crate2** into **truck2**.
    *   *Initial State:* Hoist2 is holding crate2. Truck2 is at depot2.
    *   *Result:* Crate2 is now inside truck2. Hoist2 is available again.

3.  **TruckManager Action:** The TruckManager directs **truck2** to drive from **depot2** to **distributor0**.
    *   *Initial State:* Truck2 is at depot2, carrying crate2.
    *   *Result:* Truck2 is now at distributor0, still carrying crate2.

4.  **LoadingSpecialist Action:** At **distributor0**, the LoadingSpecialist uses **hoist3** to unload **crate2** from **truck2**.
    *   *Initial State:* Truck2 is at distributor0 with crate2 inside. Hoist3 is available at distributor0.
    *   *Result:* Hoist3 is now holding crate2. Crate2 is no longer in truck2. Hoist3 is no longer available.

5.  **LoadingSpecialist Action:** At **distributor0**, the LoadingSpecialist uses **hoist3** to drop **crate2** onto **pallet3**.
    *   *Initial State:* Hoist3 is holding crate2. Pallet3 is clear at distributor0.
    *   *Result:* Crate2 is now on pallet3. Pallet3 is no longer clear. Hoist3 is available again.

**Final State:** Crate1 remains on pallet0 (as it was initially) and crate2 is now on pallet3, fulfilling the overall goal.

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan the logistics of transporting crates between a number of depots and distributors via trucks that are loaded by hoists. Depots and distributors are directly connected by roads (trucks can drive between any two depots or distributors).

A depot is a type of place.
A distributor is a type of place.
A pallet is a type of surface.
A crate is a type of surface.

Here are the actions that can be performed:

Drive a truck from one place to another place.
Use a hoist to lift a crate from a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to drop a crate to a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to load a crate into a truck at a place.
Use a hoist to unload a crate from a truck at a place.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A truck can be driven from one place to another place only if the truck is at the origin place.
Once a truck has been driven from one place to another, it is not at the origin place and is at the destination place.
A crate can be lifted by a hoist only if the hoist is at the same place as the crate, the hoist is available, and the crate is clear.
Once a crate has been lifted by a hoist from a surface at a place, the crate is not at the place, the hoist is lifting the crate, the hoist is not available, the surface is clear, and the crate is not on the surface.
A crate can be dropped by a hoist to a surface only if the hoist and surface are both at the place, the surface is clear, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been dropped by a hoist to a surface at a place, the hoist is available, the hoist is not lifting the crate, the crate is at the place, the surface is not clear, the crate is clear, and the crate is on the surface.
A crate can be loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place, the truck is at the same place, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place, 
A crate can be unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place as the truck, the hoist is available, and the crate is in the truck.
Once a crate has been unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place, the crate is not in the truck, the hoist is not available, and the hoist is lifting the crate.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, crate0 is at depot2, crate1 is at distributor0, crate2 is at depot1, hoist0 is at depot0, hoist1 is at depot1, hoist2 is at depot2, hoist3 is at distributor0, pallet0 is at depot0, pallet1 is at depot1, pallet2 is at depot2, pallet3 is at distributor0, truck0 is at depot1, truck1 is at depot1, truck2 is at depot1, hoist0 is available, hoist1 is available, hoist2 is available, hoist3 is available, crate0 is clear, crate1 is clear, crate2 is clear, pallet0 is clear, crate0 is on pallet2, crate1 is on pallet3 and crate2 is on pallet1.
My goal is to have that crate0 is on pallet0, crate1 is on pallet1 and crate2 is on pallet3.

My plan is as follows:

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan the logistics of transporting crates between a number of depots and distributors via trucks that are loaded by hoists. Depots and distributors are directly connected by roads (trucks can drive between any two depots or distributors).

A depot is a type of place.
A distributor is a type of place.
A pallet is a type of surface.
A crate is a type of surface.

Here are the actions that can be performed:

Drive a truck from one place to another place.
Use a hoist to lift a crate from a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to drop a crate to a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to load a crate into a truck at a place.
Use a hoist to unload a crate from a truck at a place.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A truck can be driven from one place to another place only if the truck is at the origin place.
Once a truck has been driven from one place to another, it is not at the origin place and is at the destination place.
A crate can be lifted by a hoist only if the hoist is at the same place as the crate, the hoist is available, and the crate is clear.
Once a crate has been lifted by a hoist from a surface at a place, the crate is not at the place, the hoist is lifting the crate, the hoist is not available, the surface is clear, and the crate is not on the surface.
A crate can be dropped by a hoist to a surface only if the hoist and surface are both at the place, the surface is clear, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been dropped by a hoist to a surface at a place, the hoist is available, the hoist is not lifting the crate, the crate is at the place, the surface is not clear, the crate is clear, and the crate is on the surface.
A crate can be loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place, the truck is at the same place, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place, 
A crate can be unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place as the truck, the hoist is available, and the crate is in the truck.
Once a crate has been unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place, the crate is not in the truck, the hoist is not available, and the hoist is lifting the crate.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, crate0 is at depot0, crate1 is at depot0, crate2 is at depot1, hoist0 is at depot0, hoist1 is at depot1, hoist2 is at depot2, hoist3 is at distributor0, pallet0 is at depot0, pallet1 is at depot1, pallet2 is at depot2, pallet3 is at distributor0, truck0 is at depot2, truck1 is at depot1, truck2 is at depot2, hoist0 is available, hoist1 is available, hoist2 is available, hoist3 is available, crate1 is clear, crate2 is clear, pallet2 is clear, pallet3 is clear, crate0 is on pallet0, crate1 is on crate0 and crate2 is on pallet1.
My goal is to have that crate0 is on pallet1, crate1 is on crate0 and crate2 is on pallet0.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]

    "pddl_domain": "(define (domain depots)\n  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)\n  (:types\n    place\n    depot distributor - place\n    surface\n    pallet crate - surface\n    hoist truck\n  )\n  (:predicates\n    (at ?obj - (or crate hoist truck pallet) ?loc - place)\n    (on ?c - crate ?s - surface)\n    (clear ?s - surface)\n    (available ?h - hoist)\n    (lifting ?h - hoist ?c - crate)\n    (in ?c - crate ?t - truck)\n  )\n\n  ;; Drive a truck from one place to another place.\n  (:action drive_truck\n    :parameters (?truck - truck ?from - place ?to - place)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at ?truck ?from)\n      (not (at ?truck ?to)) ; Truck must not already be at destination\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at ?truck ?from))\n      (at ?truck ?to)\n    )\n  )\n\n  ;; Use a hoist to lift a crate from a surface at a place.\n  (:action lift_crate_from_surface\n    :parameters (?hoist - hoist ?crate - crate ?surface - surface ?place - place)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at ?hoist ?place)\n      (at ?crate ?place)\n      (on ?crate ?surface)\n      (available ?hoist)\n      (clear ?crate) ; The crate itself must be clear to be lifted\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at ?crate ?place))\n      (not (on ?crate ?surface))\n      (clear ?surface) ; The surface it was on becomes clear\n      (not (available ?hoist))\n      (not (clear ?crate)) ; The target surface must be clear\n      (lifting ?hoist ?crate) ; Hoist must be lifting the crate\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (lifting ?hoist ?crate))\n      (available ?hoist)\n      (at ?crate ?place)\n      (on ?crate ?surface)\n      (not (clear ?surface)) ; The target surface is no longer clear\n      (clear ?crate) ; The crate itself becomes clear after being dropped\n    )\n  )\n\n  ;; Use a hoist to load a crate into a truck at a place.\n  (:action load_crate_into_truck\n    :parameters (?hoist - hoist ?crate - crate ?truck - truck ?place - place)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at ?hoist ?place)\n      (at ?truck ?place)\n      (lifting ?hoist ?crate) ; Hoist must be available to perform unload\n      (in ?crate ?truck)\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (in ?crate ?truck))\n      (not (available ?hoist))\n      (lifting ?hoist ?crate) ; Hoist is now lifting the crate\n      (not (clear ?crate)) ;
